2. It throws a `KeyError: 'chromatogram_count'` when trying to get the chromatogram count
3. The error occurs when using the example data file `tests/data/example.mzML`
4. The stack trace shows the error happens at line 407 in `run.py`: `return self.info["chromatogram_count"]`
5. Expected behavior is either (a) count of chromatograms or (b) human readable exception message about API misuse

Key technical details:
- Method name: `get_chromatogram_count()`
- File involved: `run.py` 
- Error location: line 407
- Missing key: `'chromatogram_count'` in `self.info` dictionary
- Test file: `tests/data/example.mzML`

The problem seems to be that the `info` dictionary doesn't contain the `'chromatogram_count'` key, which suggests either:
1. The key is not being populated during initialization
2. The method is being called before the key is populated
3. The key name is incorrect
4. Chromatogram counting logic is missing

1. Import pymzml
2. Create a Reader instance with an mzML file path
3. Call `reader.get_chromatogram_count()`
4. Observe the KeyError

    70	*******
    71	Summary
    72	*******
    73	
    74	pymzML is an extension to Python that offers
    75	    * a) easy access to mass spectrometry (MS) data that allows the rapid development of tools
    76	    * b) a very fast parser for mzML data, the standard mass spectrometry data format
    77	    * c) a set of functions to compare and/or handle spectra
    78	    * d) random access in compressed files
    79	    * e) interactive data visualization
    80	
    81	**************
    82	Implementation
    83	**************
    84	
    85	pymzML requires Python3.4+.
    86	The module is freely available on pymzml.github.com or pypi,
    87	published under GPL and requires no additional modules to be installed, but can
    88	optionally use numpy.
    89	
    90	
    91	********
    92	Download
    93	********
    94	
    95	Get the latest version via github
    96	    | https://github.com/pymzml/pymzML
    97

    99	    | http://pymzml.github.com/dist/pymzml.pdf

   312	        and extra Accessions.
   313	
   314	        Returns:
   315	            obo_translator (OboTranslator): translator class to translate

   325	
   326	    def _init_iter(self):
   327	        """
   328	        Initalize the iterator for the spectra and sets it to the start
   329	        of the spectrumList element.
   330	
   331	        Returns:
   332	            mzml_iter (xml.etree.ElementTree._IterParseIterator): Iterator over
   333	                all element in the file starting with the first spectrum
   334	        """
   335	        mzml_iter = iter(
   336	            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
   337	        )  # NOTE: end might be sufficient
   338	        _, self.root = next(mzml_iter)
   339	        while True:
   340	            event, element = next(mzml_iter, ("END", "END"))
   341	            if element.tag.endswith("}mzML"):
   342	                if "version" in element.attrib and len(element.attrib["version"]) > 0:
   343	                    self.info["mzml_version"] = element.attrib["version"]
   344	                else:
   345	                    s = element.attrib[
   346	                        "{http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance}" "schemaLocation"
   347	                    ]
   348	                    self.info["mzml_version"] = re.search(
   349	                        r"[0-9]*\.[0-9]*\.[0-9]*", s
   350	                    ).group()
   351	            elif element.tag.endswith("}cv"):
   352	                if (
   353	                    not self.info["obo_version"]
   354	                    and element.attrib.get("id", None) == "MS"
   355	                ):
   356	                    obo_in_mzml = element.attrib.get("version", "1.1.0")
   357	                    self.info["obo_version"] = self._obo_version_validator(obo_in_mzml)
   358	
   359	            elif element.tag.endswith("}referenceableParamGroupList"):
   360	                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list"] = True
   361	                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list_element"] = element
   362	            elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrumList"):
   363	                spec_cnt = element.attrib.get("count")
   364	                self.info["spectrum_count"] = int(spec_cnt) if spec_cnt else None
   365	                break
   366	            elif element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
   367	                chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
   368	                if chrom_cnt is None:
   369	                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
   370	                else:
   371	                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = int(chrom_cnt)
   372	                break
   373	            elif element.tag.endswith("}run"):
   374	                run_id = element.attrib.get("id")
   375	                start_time = element.attrib.get("startTimeStamp")
   376	                self.info["run_id"] = run_id
   377	                self.info["start_time"] = start_time
   378	            else:
   379	                pass
   380	        self.root.clear()
   381	        return mzml_iter
   382

Let me look at the flow more carefully:<tool_call>think<arg_key>thought</arg_key><arg_value>Looking at the code in `_init_iter`, I can see the logic:

1. It iterates through the XML elements
2. When it finds `}spectrumList`, it extracts the count and breaks (line 365)
3. When it finds `}chromatogramList`, it extracts the count and breaks (line 372)

The issue is that the method breaks when it finds EITHER spectrumList OR chromatogramList. Since spectrumList comes before chromatogramList in the XML file, when it encounters spectrumList first, it extracts the spectrum count and breaks, never reaching the chromatogramList processing.

This means `self.info["chromatogram_count"]` is never set, leading to the KeyError when `get_chromatogram_count()` tries to access it.

"""
Script to reproduce the get_chromatogram_count() issue.
"""

import pymzml
import os

def test_get_chromatogram_count():
    """Test that reproduces the KeyError issue."""
    current_dir = os.getcwd()
    mzml_file_path = os.path.join(current_dir, 'tests', 'data', 'example.mzML')
    
    print(f"Testing with file: {mzml_file_path}")
    
    try:
        reader = pymzml.run.Reader(mzml_file_path)
        print("Reader created successfully")
        
        # Check what keys are in self.info
        print("Keys in reader.info:", list(reader.info.keys()))
        
        # Try to get chromatogram count - this should fail
        chromatogram_count = reader.get_chromatogram_count()
        print(f"Chromatogram count: {chromatogram_count}")
        
    except KeyError as e:
        print(f"KeyError occurred: {e}")
        return False
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Other error occurred: {e}")
        return False
    
    return True

if __name__ == "__main__":
    success = test_get_chromatogram_count()
    if success:
        print("TEST PASSED")

## Phase 5. FIX ANALYSIS: Understanding the Problem and Solution<tool_call>task_tracker<arg_key>command</arg_key><arg_value>plan</arg_value><arg_key>task_list</arg_key><arg_value>[{"id": "problem_analysis", "title": "Analyze the root cause of the KeyError", "status": "in_progress", "notes": "The _init_iter method breaks early when it encounters spectrumList, preventing chromatogramList from being processed"}, {"id": "solution_design", "title": "Design the fix approach", "status": "todo", "notes": "Need to modify _init_iter to process both spectrumList and chromatogramList before breaking"}, {"id": "implementation", "title": "Implement the fix", "status": "todo", "notes": "Modify the _init_iter method to continue iteration until both counts are extracted"}, {"id": "testing", "title": "Test the fix comprehensively", "status": "todo", "notes": "Verify the fix works with various mzML files and edge cases"}]</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>Task list has been updated with 4 items.</tool_response><|assistant|></think>### 5.1-5.5 Fix Analysis

**Problem:** The `_init_iter` method in `run.py` breaks the parsing loop when it encounters either `spectrumList` or `chromatogramList`, whichever comes first in the XML. Since `spectrumList` typically comes before `chromatogramList`, the method never processes the chromatogram count, leaving `self.info["chromatogram_count"]` unset.

**Location:** Lines 362-372 in `/workspace/pymzml__pymzML__2.4/pymzml/run.py`

**Test Reproduction:** The reproduction script confirms that `chromatogram_count` key is missing from `self.info` after initialization.

**Best Practices:** 
- Make minimal changes to fix the core issue
- Ensure both spectrum and chromatogram counts are properly handled
- Don't break existing functionality
- Handle cases where either count might be missing

**Solution:** Modify the `_init_iter` method to continue parsing until both spectrum count and chromatogram count are extracted, or until there are no more elements to process.

        """
        Initalize the iterator for the spectra and sets it to the start
        of the spectrumList element.

        Returns:
            mzml_iter (xml.etree.ElementTree._IterParseIterator): Iterator over
                all element in the file starting with the first spectrum
        """
        mzml_iter = iter(
            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
        )  # NOTE: end might be sufficient
        _, self.root = next(mzml_iter)
        while True:
            event, element = next(mzml_iter, ("END", "END"))
            if element.tag.endswith("}mzML"):
                if "version" in element.attrib and len(element.attrib["version"]) > 0:
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = element.attrib["version"]
                else:
                    s = element.attrib[
                        "{http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance}" "schemaLocation"
                    ]
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = re.search(
                        r"[0-9]*\.[0-9]*\.[0-9]*", s
                    ).group()
            elif element.tag.endswith("}cv"):
                if (
                    not self.info["obo_version"]
                    and element.attrib.get("id", None) == "MS"
                ):
                    obo_in_mzml = element.attrib.get("version", "1.1.0")
                    self.info["obo_version"] = self._obo_version_validator(obo_in_mzml)

            elif element.tag.endswith("}referenceableParamGroupList"):
                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list"] = True
                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list_element"] = element
            elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrumList"):
                spec_cnt = element.attrib.get("count")
                self.info["spectrum_count"] = int(spec_cnt) if spec_cnt else None
                break
            elif element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                if chrom_cnt is None:
                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
                else:
                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = int(chrom_cnt)
                break
            elif element.tag.endswith("}run"):
                run_id = element.attrib.get("id")
                start_time = element.attrib.get("startTimeStamp")
                self.info["run_id"] = run_id
                self.info["start_time"] = start_time
            else:
                pass
        self.root.clear()
        return mzml_iter</arg_value><arg_key>new_str</arg_key><arg_value>    def _init_iter(self):
        """
        Initalize the iterator for the spectra and sets it to the start
        of the spectrumList element.

        Returns:
            mzml_iter (xml.etree.ElementTree._IterParseIterator): Iterator over
                all element in the file starting with the first spectrum
        """
        mzml_iter = iter(
            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
        )  # NOTE: end might be sufficient
        _, self.root = next(mzml_iter)
        # Initialize counts to None - they will be set when found
        self.info["spectrum_count"] = None
        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
        
        # Continue parsing until we find both counts or reach the end
        while True:
            event, element = next(mzml_iter, ("END", "END"))
            if event == "END" and element == "END":
                # End of file reached
                break
            if element.tag.endswith("}mzML"):
                if "version" in element.attrib and len(element.attrib["version"]) > 0:
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = element.attrib["version"]
                else:
                    s = element.attrib[
                        "{http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance}" "schemaLocation"
                    ]
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = re.search(
                        r"[0-9]*\.[0-9]*\.[0-9]*", s
                    ).group()
            elif element.tag.endswith("}cv"):
                if (
                    not self.info["obo_version"]
                    and element.attrib.get("id", None) == "MS"
                ):
                    obo_in_mzml = element.attrib.get("version", "1.1.0")
                    self.info["obo_version"] = self._obo_version_validator(obo_in_mzml)

            elif element.tag.endswith("}referenceableParamGroupList"):
                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list"] = True
                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list_element"] = element
            elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrumList"):
                spec_cnt = element.attrib.get("count")
                self.info["spectrum_count"] = int(spec_cnt) if spec_cnt else None
                # Don't break here - continue to look for chromatogramList
            elif element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                if chrom_cnt is None:
                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
                else:
                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = int(chrom_cnt)
                # Don't break here - continue to look for other elements if needed
            elif element.tag.endswith("}run"):
                run_id = element.attrib.get("id")
                start_time = element.attrib.get("startTimeStamp")
                self.info["run_id"] = run_id
                self.info["start_time"] = start_time
            else:
                pass
            
            # Break when we have both counts (or when there are no more elements)
            if (self.info["spectrum_count"] is not None and 
                self.info["chromatogram_count"] is not None):
                break
                
        self.root.clear()
        return mzml_iter</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>The file /workspace/pymzml__pymzML__2.4/pymzml/run.py has been edited. Here's the result of running `cat -n` on a snippet of /workspace/pymzml__pymzML__2.4/pymzml/run.py:

## Phase 7. VERIFICATION: Comprehensive Testing

Let's create a more comprehensive test to ensure our fix handles edge cases properly:<tool_call>str_replace_editor<arg_key>path</arg_key><arg_value>/workspace/pymzml__pymzML__2.4/comprehensive_test.py</arg_value><arg_key>command</arg_key><arg_value>create</arg_value><arg_key>file_text</arg_key><arg_value>#!/usr/bin/env python3

"""
Comprehensive test script for the get_chromatogram_count() fix.
"""

import pymzml
import os

def test_example_mzml():
    """Test with the original example file."""
    mzml_file_path = os.path.join('tests', 'data', 'example.mzML')
    
    print(f"Testing with file: {mzml_file_path}")
    
    try:
        reader = pymzml.run.Reader(mzml_file_path)
        print("Reader created successfully")
        
        # Check that both counts are present
        assert "spectrum_count" in reader.info, "spectrum_count missing from info"
        assert "chromatogram_count" in reader.info, "chromatogram_count missing from info"
        
        spectrum_count = reader.get_spectrum_count()
        chromatogram_count = reader.get_chromatogram_count()
        
        print(f"Spectrum count: {spectrum_count}")
        print(f"Chromatogram count: {chromatogram_count}")
        
        # Validate the values make sense
        assert spectrum_count == 2918, f"Expected 2918 spectra, got {spectrum_count}"
        assert chromatogram_count == 1, f"Expected 1 chromatogram, got {chromatogram_count}"
        
        print("✓ Example mzML test passed")
        return True
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"✗ Example mzML test failed: {e}")
        return False

def test_mini_chrom_mzml():
    """Test with a file that might have different characteristics."""
    mzml_file_path = os.path.join('tests', 'data', 'mini.chrom.mzML')
    
    if not os.path.exists(mzml_file_path):
        print(f"Skipping {mzml_file_path} - file not found")
        return True
    
    print(f"Testing with file: {mzml_file_path}")
    
    try:
        reader = pymzml.run.Reader(mzml_file_path)
        print("Reader created successfully")
        
        # Check that both counts are present
        assert "spectrum_count" in reader.info, "spectrum_count missing from info"
        assert "chromatogram_count" in reader.info, "chromatogram_count missing from info"
        
        spectrum_count = reader.get_spectrum_count()
        chromatogram_count = reader.get_chromatogram_count()
        
        print(f"Spectrum count: {spectrum_count}")
        print(f"Chromatogram count: {chromatogram_count}")
        
        print("✓ Mini chrom mzML test passed")
        return True
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"✗ Mini chrom mzML test failed: {e}")
        return False

def test_edge_cases():
    """Test edge cases."""
    print("Testing edge cases...")
    
    try:
        # Test that the method gracefully handles None values
        mzml_file_path = os.path.join('tests', 'data', 'example.mzML')
        reader = pymzml.run.Reader(mzml_file_path)
        
        # Both counts should be integers or None, not cause KeyErrors
        spectrum_count = reader.get_spectrum_count()
        chromatogram_count = reader.get_chromatogram_count()
        
        # They should be valid values (not KeyError)
        assert isinstance(spectrum_count, (int, type(None))), f"spectrum_count should be int or None, got {type(spectrum_count)}"
        assert isinstance(chromatogram_count, (int, type(None))), f"chromatogram_count should be int or None, got {type(chromatogram_count)}"
        
        print("✓ Edge case test passed")
        return True
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"✗ Edge case test failed: {e}")
        return False

def main():
    """Run all tests."""
    print("Running comprehensive tests for get_chromatogram_count() fix...\n")
    
    tests = [
        test_example_mzml,
        test_mini_chrom_mzml,
        test_edge_cases,
    ]
    
    passed = 0
    total = len(tests)
    
    for test in tests:
        if test():
            passed += 1
        print()
    
    print(f"Results: {passed}/{total} tests passed")
    
    if passed == total:
        print("🎉 All tests passed!")
        return True
    else:
        print("❌ Some tests failed!")
        return False

        Iterates all of the spectra in the file.

        """
        Initalize the iterator for the spectra and sets it to the start
        of the spectrumList element.

        Returns:
            mzml_iter (xml.etree.ElementTree._IterParseIterator): Iterator over
                all element in the file starting with the first spectrum
        """
        mzml_iter = iter(
            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
        )  # NOTE: end might be sufficient
        _, self.root = next(mzml_iter)
        # Initialize counts to None - they will be set when found
        self.info["spectrum_count"] = None
        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
        
        # Continue parsing until we find both counts or reach the end
        while True:
            event, element = next(mzml_iter, ("END", "END"))
            if event == "END" and element == "END":
                # End of file reached
                break
            if element.tag.endswith("}mzML"):
                if "version" in element.attrib and len(element.attrib["version"]) > 0:
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = element.attrib["version"]
                else:
                    s = element.attrib[
                        "{http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance}" "schemaLocation"
                    ]
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = re.search(
                        r"[0-9]*\.[0-9]*\.[0-9]*", s
                    ).group()
            elif element.tag.endswith("}cv"):
                if (
                    not self.info["obo_version"]
                    and element.attrib.get("id", None) == "MS"
                ):
                    obo_in_mzml = element.attrib.get("version", "1.1.0")
                    self.info["obo_version"] = self._obo_version_validator(obo_in_mzml)

                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list"] = True
                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list_element"] = element

                # Don't break here - continue to look for chromatogramList
            elif element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):

                else:
                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = int(chrom_cnt)

                start_time = element.attrib.get("startTimeStamp")

                self.info["start_time"] = start_time
            else:
                pass

                break

        """
        Initalize the iterator for the spectra and sets it to the start
        of the spectrumList element.

        Returns:
            mzml_iter (xml.etree.ElementTree._IterParseIterator): Iterator over
                all element in the file starting with the first spectrum
        """
        mzml_iter = iter(
            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
        )  # NOTE: end might be sufficient
        _, self.root = next(mzml_iter)
        # Initialize counts to None - they will be set when found
        self.info["spectrum_count"] = None
        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
        
        # We need to find both spectrumList and chromatogramList counts
        # but we must preserve the iterator position for subsequent iteration
        # Strategy: collect all elements until we find what we need, then restart iteration
        collected_elements = []
        spectrum_found = False
        chromatogram_found = False
        
        while True:
            try:
                event, element = next(mzml_iter)
                collected_elements.append((event, element))
                
                if element.tag.endswith("}mzML"):
                    if "version" in element.attrib and len(element.attrib["version"]) > 0:
                        self.info["mzml_version"] = element.attrib["version"]
                    else:
                        s = element.attrib[
                            "{http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance}" "schemaLocation"
                        ]
                        self.info["mzml_version"] = re.search(
                            r"[0-9]*\.[0-9]*\.[0-9]*", s
                        ).group()
                elif element.tag.endswith("}cv"):
                    if (
                        not self.info["obo_version"]
                        and element.attrib.get("id", None) == "MS"
                    ):
                        obo_in_mzml = element.attrib.get("version", "1.1.0")
                        self.info["obo_version"] = self._obo_version_validator(obo_in_mzml)

                elif element.tag.endswith("}referenceableParamGroupList"):
                    self.info["referenceable_param_group_list"] = True
                    self.info["referenceable_param_group_list_element"] = element
                elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrumList"):
                    spec_cnt = element.attrib.get("count")
                    self.info["spectrum_count"] = int(spec_cnt) if spec_cnt else None
                    spectrum_found = True
                elif element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                    chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                    if chrom_cnt is None:
                        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
                    else:
                        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = int(chrom_cnt)
                    chromatogram_found = True
                elif element.tag.endswith("}run"):
                    run_id = element.attrib.get("id")
                    start_time = element.attrib.get("startTimeStamp")
                    self.info["run_id"] = run_id
                    self.info["start_time"] = start_time
                    
                # Check if we have both counts or if we've gone too far
                if spectrum_found and chromatogram_found:
                    break
                # Safety check to avoid infinite loops
                if len(collected_elements) > 10000:  # reasonable limit
                    break
                    
            except StopIteration:
                break

        # This preserves the original behavior while ensuring we have both counts
        new_mzml_iter = iter(
            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
        )

Let me see if I can preserve the original behavior while ensuring both counts are captured:<tool_call>str_replace_editor<arg_key>path</arg_key><arg_value>/workspace/pymzml__pymzML__2.4/pymzml/run.py</arg_value><arg_key>new_str</arg_key><arg_value>    def _init_iter(self):
        """
        Initalize the iterator for the spectra and sets it to the start
        of the spectrumList element.

        Returns:
            mzml_iter (xml.etree.ElementTree._IterParseIterator): Iterator over
                all element in the file starting with the first spectrum
        """
        mzml_iter = iter(
            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
        )  # NOTE: end might be sufficient
        _, self.root = next(mzml_iter)
        # Initialize counts to None - they will be set when found
        self.info["spectrum_count"] = None
        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
        
        # Parse elements until we find spectrumList (original stopping condition)
        # but also look for chromatogramList along the way
        while True:
            event, element = next(mzml_iter, ("END", "END"))
            if event == "END" and element == "END":
                # End of file reached
                break
            if element.tag.endswith("}mzML"):
                if "version" in element.attrib and len(element.attrib["version"]) > 0:
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = element.attrib["version"]
                else:
                    s = element.attrib[
                        "{http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance}" "schemaLocation"
                    ]
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = re.search(
                        r"[0-9]*\.[0-9]*\.[0-9]*", s
                    ).group()
            elif element.tag.endswith("}cv"):
                if (
                    not self.info["obo_version"]
                    and element.attrib.get("id", None) == "MS"
                ):
                    obo_in_mzml = element.attrib.get("version", "1.1.0")
                    self.info["obo_version"] = self._obo_version_validator(obo_in_mzml)

            elif element.tag.endswith("}referenceableParamGroupList"):
                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list"] = True
                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list_element"] = element
            elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrumList"):
                spec_cnt = element.attrib.get("count")
                self.info["spectrum_count"] = int(spec_cnt) if spec_cnt else None
                # Found spectrumList - this is where we originally stopped
                # But we might have already found chromatogramList earlier
                break
            elif element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                if chrom_cnt is None:
                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
                else:
                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = int(chrom_cnt)
                # Don't break here - continue looking for spectrumList
                # (this is the key change from original code)
            elif element.tag.endswith("}run"):
                run_id = element.attrib.get("id")
                start_time = element.attrib.get("startTimeStamp")
                self.info["run_id"] = run_id
                self.info["start_time"] = start_time
            else:
                pass
                
        self.root.clear()
        return mzml_iter</arg_value><arg_key>old_str</arg_key><arg_value>    def _init_iter(self):
        """
        Initalize the iterator for the spectra and sets it to the start
        of the spectrumList element.

        Returns:
            mzml_iter (xml.etree.ElementTree._IterParseIterator): Iterator over
                all element in the file starting with the first spectrum
        """
        mzml_iter = iter(
            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
        )  # NOTE: end might be sufficient
        _, self.root = next(mzml_iter)
        # Initialize counts to None - they will be set when found
        self.info["spectrum_count"] = None
        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
        
        # We need to find both spectrumList and chromatogramList counts
        # but we must preserve the iterator position for subsequent iteration
        # Strategy: collect all elements until we find what we need, then restart iteration
        collected_elements = []
        spectrum_found = False
        chromatogram_found = False
        
        while True:
            try:
                event, element = next(mzml_iter)
                collected_elements.append((event, element))
                
                if element.tag.endswith("}mzML"):
                    if "version" in element.attrib and len(element.attrib["version"]) > 0:
                        self.info["mzml_version"] = element.attrib["version"]
                    else:
                        s = element.attrib[
                            "{http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance}" "schemaLocation"
                        ]
                        self.info["mzml_version"] = re.search(
                            r"[0-9]*\.[0-9]*\.[0-9]*", s
                        ).group()
                elif element.tag.endswith("}cv"):
                    if (
                        not self.info["obo_version"]
                        and element.attrib.get("id", None) == "MS"
                    ):
                        obo_in_mzml = element.attrib.get("version", "1.1.0")
                        self.info["obo_version"] = self._obo_version_validator(obo_in_mzml)

                elif element.tag.endswith("}referenceableParamGroupList"):
                    self.info["referenceable_param_group_list"] = True
                    self.info["referenceable_param_group_list_element"] = element
                elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrumList"):
                    spec_cnt = element.attrib.get("count")
                    self.info["spectrum_count"] = int(spec_cnt) if spec_cnt else None
                    spectrum_found = True
                elif element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                    chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                    if chrom_cnt is None:
                        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
                    else:
                        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = int(chrom_cnt)
                    chromatogram_found = True
                elif element.tag.endswith("}run"):
                    run_id = element.attrib.get("id")
                    start_time = element.attrib.get("startTimeStamp")
                    self.info["run_id"] = run_id
                    self.info["start_time"] = start_time
                    
                # Check if we have both counts or if we've gone too far
                if spectrum_found and chromatogram_found:
                    break
                # Safety check to avoid infinite loops
                if len(collected_elements) > 10000:  # reasonable limit
                    break
                    
            except StopIteration:
                break
                
        self.root.clear()
        
        # Now recreate the iterator to start from the beginning for proper iteration
        # This preserves the original behavior while ensuring we have both counts
        new_mzml_iter = iter(
            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
        )
        next(new_mzml_iter)  # skip the root element like before
        return new_mzml_iter</arg_value><arg_key>command</arg_key><arg_value>str_replace</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>The file /workspace/pymzml__pymzML__2.4/pymzml/run.py has been edited.         Iterator for the class :py:class:`Run`.

        Iterates all of the spectra in the file.

        """
        Initalize the iterator for the spectra and sets it to the start
        of the spectrumList element.

        Returns:
            mzml_iter (xml.etree.ElementTree._IterParseIterator): Iterator over
                all element in the file starting with the first spectrum
        """

            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))

                        "{http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance}" "schemaLocation"
                    ]
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = re.search(
                        r"[0-9]*\.[0-9]*\.[0-9]*", s
                    ).group()

                if (
                    not self.info["obo_version"]
                    and element.attrib.get("id", None) == "MS"
                ):
                    obo_in_mzml = element.attrib.get("version", "1.1.0")
                    self.info["obo_version"] = self._obo_version_validator(obo_in_mzml)

                if chromatogram_found:

            elif element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):

                start_time = element.attrib.get("startTimeStamp")

                self.info["start_time"] = start_time

        """
        Initalize the iterator for the spectra and sets it to the start
        of the spectrumList element.

        Returns:
            mzml_iter (xml.etree.ElementTree._IterParseIterator): Iterator over
                all element in the file starting with the first spectrum
        """
        mzml_iter = iter(
            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
        )  # NOTE: end might be sufficient
        _, self.root = next(mzml_iter)
        
        # Initialize counts to None - they will be set when found
        self.info["spectrum_count"] = None
        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
        
        # Store elements we've seen to reconstruct the iterator properly
        seen_elements = []
        spectrum_list_found = False
        chromatogram_list_found = False
        
        # Parse until we find both counts or reach a reasonable limit
        element_count = 0
        max_elements = 10000  # safety limit
        
        while element_count < max_elements:
            try:
                event, element = next(mzml_iter)
                element_count += 1
                seen_elements.append((event, element))
                
                if element.tag.endswith("}mzML"):
                    if "version" in element.attrib and len(element.attrib["version"]) > 0:
                        self.info["mzml_version"] = element.attrib["version"]
                    else:
                        s = element.attrib[
                            "{http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance}" "schemaLocation"
                        ]
                        self.info["mzml_version"] = re.search(
                            r"[0-9]*\.[0-9]*\.[0-9]*", s
                        ).group()
                elif element.tag.endswith("}cv"):
                    if (
                        not self.info["obo_version"]
                        and element.attrib.get("id", None) == "MS"
                    ):
                        obo_in_mzml = element.attrib.get("version", "1.1.0")
                        self.info["obo_version"] = self._obo_version_validator(obo_in_mzml)

                elif element.tag.endswith("}referenceableParamGroupList"):
                    self.info["referenceable_param_group_list"] = True
                    self.info["referenceable_param_group_list_element"] = element
                elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrumList"):
                    spec_cnt = element.attrib.get("count")
                    self.info["spectrum_count"] = int(spec_cnt) if spec_cnt else None
                    spectrum_list_found = True
                elif element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                    chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                    if chrom_cnt is None:
                        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
                    else:
                        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = int(chrom_cnt)
                    chromatogram_list_found = True
                elif element.tag.endswith("}run"):
                    run_id = element.attrib.get("id")
                    start_time = element.attrib.get("startTimeStamp")
                    self.info["run_id"] = run_id
                    self.info["start_time"] = start_time
                
                # If we found both counts, we can stop scanning
                if spectrum_list_found and chromatogram_list_found:
                    break
                    
            except StopIteration:
                break
        
        self.root.clear()
        
        # Create a new iterator that starts from the beginning
        # This ensures proper positioning for subsequent iteration
        fresh_iter = iter(
            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
        )
        next(fresh_iter)  # Skip root element
        return fresh_iter</arg_value><arg_key>old_str</arg_key><arg_value>    def _init_iter(self):

                elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrumList"):
                    spec_cnt = element.attrib.get("count")
                    self.info["spectrum_count"] = int(spec_cnt) if spec_cnt else None
                    spectrum_list_found = True
                elif element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                    chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                    if chrom_cnt is None:
                        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
                    else:
                        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = int(chrom_cnt)
                    chromatogram_list_found = True
                elif element.tag.endswith("}run"):
                    run_id = element.attrib.get("id")
                    start_time = element.attrib.get("startTimeStamp")
                    self.info["run_id"] = run_id
                    self.info["start_time"] = start_time
                
                # If we found both counts, we can stop scanning
                if spectrum_list_found and chromatogram_list_found:
                    break
                    
            except StopIteration:
                break
        
        self.root.clear()
        
        # Create a new iterator that starts from the beginning
        # This ensures proper positioning for subsequent iteration
        fresh_iter = iter(
            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
        )
        next(fresh_iter)  # Skip root element
        return fresh_iter</arg_value><arg_key>command</arg_key><arg_value>str_replace</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>The file /workspace/pymzml__pymzML__2.4/pymzml/run.py has been edited.     def __next__(self):
        """
        Iterator for the class :py:class:`Run`.

        Iterates all of the spectra in the file.

        Returns:
            Spectrum (:py:class:`Spectrum`): a spectrum object with interface
                to the original spectrum element.

        Example:

        >>> for spectrum in Reader:
        ...     print(spectrum.mz, end='\\r')

        """
        has_ref_group = self.info.get("referenceable_param_group_list", False)
        while True:
            event, element = next(self.iter, ("END", "END"))
            if event == "end":
                if element.tag.endswith("}spectrum"):
                    spectrum = spec.Spectrum(element)
                    if has_ref_group:
                        spectrum._set_params_from_reference_group(
                            self.info["referenceable_param_group_list_element"]
                        )
                    ms_level = spectrum.ms_level
                    spectrum.measured_precision = self.ms_precisions[ms_level]
                    spectrum.calling_instance = self
                    return spectrum
                if element.tag.endswith("}chromatogram"):
                    if self.skip_chromatogram:
                        continue
                    spectrum = spec.Chromatogram(element)
                    # if has_ref_group:
                    #     spectrum._set_params_from_reference_group(
                    #         self.info['referenceable_param_group_list_element']
                    #     )
                    spectrum.calling_instance = self
                    return spectrum
            elif event == "END":

        """
        Initalize the iterator for the spectra and sets it to the start
        of the spectrumList element.

        Returns:
            mzml_iter (xml.etree.ElementTree._IterParseIterator): Iterator over
                all element in the file starting with the first spectrum
        """
        # Initialize counts with default None values
        self.info["spectrum_count"] = None
        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None

            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
        )  # NOTE: end might be sufficient
        _, self.root = next(mzml_iter)

            # Check for end of iteration

                break

                if "version" in element.attrib and len(element.attrib["version"]) > 0:
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = element.attrib["version"]
                else:
                    s = element.attrib[
                        "{http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance}" "schemaLocation"
                    ]
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = re.search(
                        r"[0-9]*\.[0-9]*\.[0-9]*", s
                    ).group()

                    not self.info["obo_version"]
                    and element.attrib.get("id", None) == "MS"
                ):
                    obo_in_mzml = element.attrib.get("version", "1.1.0")
                    self.info["obo_version"] = self._obo_version_validator(obo_in_mzml)

                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list_element"] = element

                spec_cnt = element.attrib.get("count")
                self.info["spectrum_count"] = int(spec_cnt) if spec_cnt else None

                break

                start_time = element.attrib.get("startTimeStamp")
                self.info["run_id"] = run_id
                self.info["start_time"] = start_time
            else:
                pass
        self.root.clear()

        """
        Number of chromatograms in file.

        Returns:
            chromatogram count (int): Number of chromatograms in file.
        """
        # If we already found the count during initialization, return it
        if "chromatogram_count" in self.info:
            return self.info["chromatogram_count"]
        
        # Otherwise, we need to scan the file to find the count
        # This is a lazy approach to avoid breaking the main initialization logic
        try:
            # Create a temporary iterator to scan for chromatogramList
            temp_iter = iter(
                ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end",))
            )
            # Skip the root element
            next(temp_iter)

                event, element = next(temp_iter)
                if element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                    chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                    count = int(chrom_cnt) if chrom_cnt else None
                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = count
                    return count
                # Stop scanning after a reasonable number of elements to avoid performance issues
                elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrumList"):
                    # If we reach spectrumList, chromatogramList should have been found already
                    break
        except StopIteration:
            pass
        except Exception:
            pass

        """
        Initalize the iterator for the spectra and sets it to the start
        of the spectrumList element.

        Returns:
            mzml_iter (xml.etree.ElementTree._IterParseIterator): Iterator over
                all element in the file starting with the first spectrum
        """
        # Initialize counts with default None values
        self.info["spectrum_count"] = None
        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
        
        # First, scan the header to find both counts without affecting main iterator
        self._scan_header_for_counts()
        
        # Now create the main iterator for iteration
        mzml_iter = iter(
            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
        )  # NOTE: end might be sufficient
        _, self.root = next(mzml_iter)
        while True:
            event, element = next(mzml_iter, ("END", "END"))
            # Check for end of iteration
            if event == "END" and element == "END":
                break
            if element.tag.endswith("}mzML"):
                if "version" in element.attrib and len(element.attrib["version"]) > 0:
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = element.attrib["version"]
                else:
                    s = element.attrib[
                        "{http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance}" "schemaLocation"
                    ]
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = re.search(
                        r"[0-9]*\.[0-9]*\.[0-9]*", s
                    ).group()
            elif element.tag.endswith("}cv"):
                if (
                    not self.info["obo_version"]
                    and element.attrib.get("id", None) == "MS"
                ):
                    obo_in_mzml = element.attrib.get("version", "1.1.0")
                    self.info["obo_version"] = self._obo_version_validator(obo_in_mzml)

            elif element.tag.endswith("}referenceableParamGroupList"):
                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list"] = True
                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list_element"] = element
            elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrumList"):
                # We already got the count during header scan, just break to preserve iterator
                break
            elif element.tag.endswith("}run"):
                run_id = element.attrib.get("id")
                start_time = element.attrib.get("startTimeStamp")
                self.info["run_id"] = run_id
                self.info["start_time"] = start_time
            else:
                pass
        self.root.clear()
        return mzml_iter

    def _scan_header_for_counts(self):
        """
        Scan the file header to find spectrum and chromatogram counts.
        This is done in a separate pass to avoid affecting the main iterator.
        """
        try:
            # Create a temporary iterator for scanning
            scan_iter = iter(
                ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end",))
            )
            # Skip the root element
            next(scan_iter)
            
            spectrum_found = False
            chromatogram_found = False
            
            while True:
                event, element = next(scan_iter)
                if element.tag.endswith("}spectrumList"):
                    spec_cnt = element.attrib.get("count")
                    self.info["spectrum_count"] = int(spec_cnt) if spec_cnt else None
                    spectrum_found = True
                elif element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                    chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = int(chrom_cnt) if chrom_cnt else None
                    chromatogram_found = True
                
                # Stop when we find both or when we get too far into the file
                if (spectrum_found and chromatogram_found) or element.tag.endswith("}spectrum"):
                    break
                    
        except StopIteration:
            pass
        except Exception:
            # If there's any error in scanning, we just continue with defaults (None)

                else:
                    s = element.attrib[
                        "{http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance}" "schemaLocation"
                    ]
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = re.search(
                        r"[0-9]*\.[0-9]*\.[0-9]*", s

                    not self.info["obo_version"]
                    and element.attrib.get("id", None) == "MS"

                    self.info["obo_version"] = self._obo_version_validator(obo_in_mzml)

                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list_element"] = element

                spec_cnt = element.attrib.get("count")
                self.info["spectrum_count"] = int(spec_cnt) if spec_cnt else None

                break

                self.info["start_time"] = start_time
            else:
                pass

        """
        Number of chromatograms in file.

        Returns:
            chromatogram count (int): Number of chromatograms in file.
        """
        # If we already found the count during initialization, return it
        if "chromatogram_count" in self.info:
            return self.info["chromatogram_count"]
        
        # Otherwise, we need to scan the file to find the count
        # This is a lazy approach to avoid breaking the main initialization logic
        try:
            # Create a temporary iterator to scan for chromatogramList
            temp_iter = iter(
                ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end",))
            )
            # Skip the root element
            next(temp_iter)
            
            while True:
                event, element = next(temp_iter)
                if element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                    chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                    count = int(chrom_cnt) if chrom_cnt else None
                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = count
                    return count
                # Stop scanning after a reasonable number of elements to avoid performance issues
                elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrumList"):
                    # If we reach spectrumList, chromatogramList should have been found already
                    break
        except StopIteration:
            pass
        except Exception:
            pass
        
        # If not found, store and return None
        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
        return None</arg_value><arg_key>command</arg_key><arg_value>str_replace</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>The file /workspace/pymzml__pymzML__2.4/pymzml/run.py has been edited.    340	            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
   341	        )  # NOTE: end might be sufficient
   342	        _, self.root = next(mzml_iter)
   343	        while True:
   344	            event, element = next(mzml_iter, ("END", "END"))
   345	            # Check for end of iteration
   346	            if event == "END" and element == "END":
   347	                break
   348	            if element.tag.endswith("}mzML"):
   349	                if "version" in element.attrib and len(element.attrib["version"]) > 0:
   350	                    self.info["mzml_version"] = element.attrib["version"]
   351	                else:
   352	                    s = element.attrib[
   353	                        "{http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance}" "schemaLocation"
   354	                    ]
   355	                    self.info["mzml_version"] = re.search(
   356	                        r"[0-9]*\.[0-9]*\.[0-9]*", s
   357	                    ).group()
   358	            elif element.tag.endswith("}cv"):
   359	                if (
   360	                    not self.info["obo_version"]
   361	                    and element.attrib.get("id", None) == "MS"
   362	                ):
   363	                    obo_in_mzml = element.attrib.get("version", "1.1.0")
   364	                    self.info["obo_version"] = self._obo_version_validator(obo_in_mzml)
   365	
   366	            elif element.tag.endswith("}referenceableParamGroupList"):
   367	                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list"] = True
   368	                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list_element"] = element
   369	            elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrumList"):
   370	                spec_cnt = element.attrib.get("count")
   371	                self.info["spectrum_count"] = int(spec_cnt) if spec_cnt else None
   372	                # If we already found chromatogramList during initialization, we can break
   373	                if self.info.get("chromatogram_count") is not None:
   374	                    break
   375	                # Otherwise continue looking for chromatogramList (don't break yet)
   376	            elif element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
   377	                chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
   378	                if chrom_cnt is None:
   379	                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
   380	                else:
   381	                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = int(chrom_cnt)
   382	                # If we already found spectrumList, we can break
   383	                if self.info.get("spectrum_count") is not None:
   384	                    break
   385	                # Otherwise continue looking for spectrumList
   386	            elif element.tag.endswith("}run"):
   387	                run_id = element.attrib.get("id")
   388	                start_time = element.attrib.get("startTimeStamp")
   389	                self.info["run_id"] = run_id
   390	                self.info["start_time"] = start_time
   391	            else:
   392	                pass
   393	        self.root.clear()
   394	        return mzml_iter
   395	
   396	    def __iter__(self):
   397	        """Return self."""
   398	        return self
   399

import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

# Let's manually parse to see the structure more carefully
with open('tests/data/example.mzML', 'rb') as f:
    parser = ET.iterparse(f, events=('end', 'start'))
    root_event, root_element = next(parser)
    print('Root:', root_event, root_element.tag)
    
    count = 0
    spectrum_list_found = False
    chromatogram_list_found = False
    for event, element in parser:
        count += 1
        if element.tag.endswith('}mzML'):
            print(f'{count}: Found inner mzML element')
        elif element.tag.endswith('}spectrumList'):
            print(f'{count}: Found spectrumList, count={element.attrib.get(\"count\")}')
            spectrum_list_found = True
        elif element.tag.endswith('}chromatogramList'):  
            print(f'{count}: Found chromatogramList, count={element.attrib.get(\"count\")}')
            chromatogram_list_found = True
        elif element.tag.endswith('}spectrum'):
            print(f'{count}: Found first spectrum')
            break
            
        if spectrum_list_found and chromatogram_list_found:
            print(f'{count}: Both found, continuing to see what happens...')
            
        if count > 1000:  # safety break
            break
    print(f'Total elements processed: {count}')

        """
        Number of chromatograms in file.

        Returns:
            chromatogram count (int): Number of chromatograms in file.
        """
        # If we already found the count during initialization, return it
        if "chromatogram_count" in self.info:
            return self.info["chromatogram_count"]
        
        # Otherwise, we need to scan the file to find the count
        # This is a lazy approach to avoid breaking the main initialization logic
        try:
            # Open a new file handle to scan for chromatogramList
            # We need to use the same encoding and file handling logic
            file_path = self.info.get("file_name")

                import xml.etree.ElementTree as ElementTree
                # Create a temporary file handler using the same logic as _open_file
                temp_file_obj = self._open_file(file_path)
                temp_iter = iter(ElementTree.iterparse(temp_file_obj, events=("end",)))
                # Skip the root element
                next(temp_iter)
                
                while True:
                    event, element = next(temp_iter)
                    if element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                        chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                        count = int(chrom_cnt) if chrom_cnt else None
                        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = count
                        temp_file_obj.close()
                        return count
                    # Stop scanning after a reasonable number of elements to avoid performance issues
                    elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrum"):
                        # If we reach spectrum, chromatogramList should have been found already
                        break
                temp_file_obj.close()
        except Exception:
            pass
        
        # If not found, store and return None
        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None

        """
        Initalize the iterator for the spectra and sets it to the start
        of the spectrumList element.

        Returns:
            mzml_iter (xml.etree.ElementTree._IterParseIterator): Iterator over
                all element in the file starting with the first spectrum
        """
        mzml_iter = iter(
            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
        )  # NOTE: end might be sufficient
        _, self.root = next(mzml_iter)
        while True:
            event, element = next(mzml_iter, ("END", "END"))
            if element.tag.endswith("}mzML"):
                if "version" in element.attrib and len(element.attrib["version"]) > 0:
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = element.attrib["version"]
                else:
                    s = element.attrib[
                        "{http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance}" "schemaLocation"
                    ]
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = re.search(
                        r"[0-9]*\.[0-9]*\.[0-9]*", s
                    ).group()
            elif element.tag.endswith("}cv"):
                if (
                    not self.info["obo_version"]
                    and element.attrib.get("id", None) == "MS"
                ):
                    obo_in_mzml = element.attrib.get("version", "1.1.0")
                    self.info["obo_version"] = self._obo_version_validator(obo_in_mzml)

            elif element.tag.endswith("}referenceableParamGroupList"):
                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list"] = True
                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list_element"] = element
            elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrumList"):
                spec_cnt = element.attrib.get("count")
                self.info["spectrum_count"] = int(spec_cnt) if spec_cnt else None
                break
            elif element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                if chrom_cnt is None:
                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
                else:
                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = int(chrom_cnt)
                break
            elif element.tag.endswith("}run"):
                run_id = element.attrib.get("id")
                start_time = element.attrib.get("startTimeStamp")
                self.info["run_id"] = run_id
                self.info["start_time"] = start_time
            else:
                pass
        self.root.clear()
        return mzml_iter</arg_value><arg_key>old_str</arg_key><arg_value>    def _init_iter(self):
        """
        Initalize the iterator for the spectra and sets it to the start
        of the spectrumList element.

        Returns:
            mzml_iter (xml.etree.ElementTree._IterParseIterator): Iterator over
                all element in the file starting with the first spectrum
        """
        # Initialize counts with default None values
        self.info["spectrum_count"] = None
        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
        
        mzml_iter = iter(
            ElementTree.iterparse(self.info["file_object"], events=("end", "start"))
        )  # NOTE: end might be sufficient
        _, self.root = next(mzml_iter)
        while True:
            event, element = next(mzml_iter, ("END", "END"))
            # Check for end of iteration
            if event == "END" and element == "END":
                break
            if element.tag.endswith("}mzML"):
                if "version" in element.attrib and len(element.attrib["version"]) > 0:
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = element.attrib["version"]
                else:
                    s = element.attrib[
                        "{http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance}" "schemaLocation"
                    ]
                    self.info["mzml_version"] = re.search(
                        r"[0-9]*\.[0-9]*\.[0-9]*", s
                    ).group()
            elif element.tag.endswith("}cv"):
                if (
                    not self.info["obo_version"]
                    and element.attrib.get("id", None) == "MS"
                ):
                    obo_in_mzml = element.attrib.get("version", "1.1.0")
                    self.info["obo_version"] = self._obo_version_validator(obo_in_mzml)

            elif element.tag.endswith("}referenceableParamGroupList"):
                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list"] = True
                self.info["referenceable_param_group_list_element"] = element
            elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrumList"):
                spec_cnt = element.attrib.get("count")
                self.info["spectrum_count"] = int(spec_cnt) if spec_cnt else None
                # If we already found chromatogramList during initialization, we can break
                if self.info.get("chromatogram_count") is not None:
                    break
                # Otherwise continue looking for chromatogramList (don't break yet)
            elif element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                if chrom_cnt is None:
                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
                else:
                    self.info["chromatogram_count"] = int(chrom_cnt)
                # If we already found spectrumList, we can break
                if self.info.get("spectrum_count") is not None:
                    break
                # Otherwise continue looking for spectrumList
            elif element.tag.endswith("}run"):
                run_id = element.attrib.get("id")
                start_time = element.attrib.get("startTimeStamp")
                self.info["run_id"] = run_id
                self.info["start_time"] = start_time
            else:
                pass
        self.root.clear()
        return mzml_iter</arg_value><arg_key>command</arg_key><arg_value>str_replace</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>The file /workspace/pymzml__pymzML__2.4/pymzml/run.py has been edited. Let me fix the lazy loading function:<tool_call>str_replace_editor<arg_key>path</arg_key><arg_value>/workspace/pymzml__pymzML__2.4/pymzml/run.py</arg_value><arg_key>new_str</arg_key><arg_value>    def get_chromatogram_count(self):
        """
        Number of chromatograms in file.

        Returns:
            chromatogram count (int): Number of chromatograms in file.
        """
        # If we already found the count during initialization, return it
        if "chromatogram_count" in self.info:
            return self.info["chromatogram_count"]
        
        # Otherwise, we need to scan the file to find the count
        # This is a lazy approach to avoid breaking the main initialization logic
        try:
            # Open a new file handle to scan for chromatogramList
            # We need to use the same encoding and file handling logic
            file_path = self.info.get("file_name")
            if file_path:
                import xml.etree.ElementTree as ElementTree
                # Create a temporary file handler using the same logic as _open_file
                temp_file_obj = self._open_file(file_path)
                temp_iter = iter(ElementTree.iterparse(temp_file_obj, events=("end",)))
                # Skip the root element
                next(temp_iter)
                
                while True:
                    event, element = next(temp_iter)
                    if element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                        chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                        count = int(chrom_cnt) if chrom_cnt else None
                        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = count
                        temp_file_obj.close()
                        return count
                    # Stop scanning when we get well into the data section to avoid performance issues
                    elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrum") and event == "end":
                        # We've passed the header section, chromatogramList should have been found
                        break
                temp_file_obj.close()
        except Exception:
            pass
        
        # If not found, store and return None
        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None

        """
        Number of chromatograms in file.

        Returns:
            chromatogram count (int): Number of chromatograms in file.
        """
        # If we already found the count during initialization, return it
        if "chromatogram_count" in self.info:
            return self.info["chromatogram_count"]
        
        # Otherwise, we need to scan the file to find the count
        # This is a lazy approach to avoid breaking the main initialization logic
        try:
            # Open a new file handle to scan for chromatogramList
            # We need to use the same encoding and file handling logic
            file_path = self.info.get("file_name")
            if file_path:
                import xml.etree.ElementTree as ElementTree
                # Create a temporary file handler using the same logic as _open_file
                temp_file_obj = self._open_file(file_path)
                temp_iter = iter(ElementTree.iterparse(temp_file_obj, events=("end",)))
                # Skip the root element
                next(temp_iter)
                
                while True:
                    event, element = next(temp_iter)
                    if element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                        chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                        count = int(chrom_cnt) if chrom_cnt else None
                        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = count
                        temp_file_obj.close()
                        return count
                    # Stop scanning after a reasonable number of elements to avoid performance issues
                    elif element.tag.endswith("}spectrum"):
                        # If we reach spectrum, chromatogramList should have been found already
                        break
                temp_file_obj.close()
        except Exception:
            pass
        
        # If not found, store and return None
        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
        return None</arg_value><arg_key>command</arg_key><arg_value>str_replace</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>The file /workspace/pymzml__pymzML__2.4/pymzml/run.py has been edited. import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

# Parse with both start and end events to see the full structure
with open('tests/data/example.mzML', 'rb') as f:
    parser = ET.iterparse(f, events=('start', 'end'))
    root_event, root_element = next(parser)
    print('Root:', root_element.tag)
    
    count = 0
    for event, element in parser:
        count += 1
        if element.tag.endswith('}spectrumList') and event == 'start':
            print(f'{count}: START spectrumList')
        elif element.tag.endswith('}spectrumList') and event == 'end':
            print(f'{count}: END spectrumList')
        elif element.tag.endswith('}chromatogramList') and event == 'start':
            print(f'{count}: START chromatogramList, count={element.attrib.get(\"count\")}')
        elif element.tag.endswith('}chromatogramList') and event == 'end':
            print(f'{count}: END chromatogramList')
        elif element.tag.endswith('}spectrum') and event == 'start':
            print(f'{count}: START first spectrum')
            break
        if count > 1000:
            break

        """
        Number of chromatograms in file.

        Returns:
            chromatogram count (int): Number of chromatograms in file.
        """
        # If we already found the count during initialization, return it
        if "chromatogram_count" in self.info:
            return self.info["chromatogram_count"]
        
        # Otherwise, we need to scan the file to find the count
        # This is a lazy approach to avoid breaking the main initialization logic
        try:
            # Open a new file handle to scan for chromatogramList
            # We need to use the same encoding and file handling logic
            file_path = self.info.get("file_name")
            if file_path:
                import xml.etree.ElementTree as ElementTree
                # Create a temporary file handler using the same logic as _open_file
                temp_file_obj = self._open_file(file_path)
                temp_iter = iter(ElementTree.iterparse(temp_file_obj, events=("end",)))
                # Skip the root element
                next(temp_iter)
                
                while True:
                    event, element = next(temp_iter)
                    if element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                        chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                        count = int(chrom_cnt) if chrom_cnt else None
                        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = count
                        temp_file_obj.close()
                        return count
                    # In mzML files, the chromatogramList is typically at the end,
                    # but we should stop after parsing a reasonable portion to avoid performance issues
                    # Let's parse up to a large but safe number of elements
                    elif event == "end" and element.tag.endswith("}indexList"):
                        # We've reached the index section, chromatogramList should have been found
                        break
                temp_file_obj.close()
        except Exception:
            pass
        
        # If not found, store and return None
        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None

        """
        Number of chromatograms in file.

        Returns:
            chromatogram count (int): Number of chromatograms in file.
        """
        # If we already found the count during initialization, return it
        if "chromatogram_count" in self.info:
            return self.info["chromatogram_count"]
        
        # Otherwise, we need to scan the file to find the count
        # This is a lazy approach to avoid breaking the main initialization logic
        try:
            # Open a new file handle to scan for chromatogramList
            # We need to use the same encoding and file handling logic
            file_path = self.info.get("file_name")
            if file_path:
                import xml.etree.ElementTree as ElementTree
                # Create a temporary file handler using the same logic as _open_file
                temp_file_obj = self._open_file(file_path)
                temp_iter = iter(ElementTree.iterparse(temp_file_obj, events=("end",)))
                # Skip the root element
                next(temp_iter)
                
                while True:
                    event, element = next(temp_iter)
                    if element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
                        chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
                        count = int(chrom_cnt) if chrom_cnt else None
                        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = count
                        temp_file_obj.close()
                        return count

                        # We've passed the header section, chromatogramList should have been found
                        break
                temp_file_obj.close()

            pass

   408	        if "chromatogram_count" in self.info:
   409	            return self.info["chromatogram_count"]
   410	        
   411	        # Otherwise, we need to scan the file to find the count
   412	        # This is a lazy approach to avoid breaking the main initialization logic
   413	        try:
   414	            # Open a new file handle to scan for chromatogramList
   415	            # We need to use the same encoding and file handling logic
   416	            file_path = self.info.get("file_name")
   417	            if file_path:
   418	                import xml.etree.ElementTree as ElementTree
   419	                # Create a temporary file handler using the same logic as _open_file
   420	                temp_file_obj = self._open_file(file_path)
   421	                temp_iter = iter(ElementTree.iterparse(temp_file_obj, events=("end",)))
   422	                # Skip the root element
   423	                next(temp_iter)
   424	                
   425	                while True:
   426	                    event, element = next(temp_iter)
   427	                    if element.tag.endswith("}chromatogramList"):
   428	                        chrom_cnt = element.attrib.get("count", None)
   429	                        count = int(chrom_cnt) if chrom_cnt else None
   430	                        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = count
   431	                        temp_file_obj.close()
   432	                        return count
   433	                    # In mzML files, the chromatogramList is typically at the end,
   434	                    # but we should stop after parsing a reasonable portion to avoid performance issues
   435	                    # Let's parse up to a large but safe number of elements
   436	                    elif event == "end" and element.tag.endswith("}indexList"):
   437	                        # We've reached the index section, chromatogramList should have been found
   438	                        break
   439	                temp_file_obj.close()
   440	        except Exception:
   441	            pass
   442	        
   443	        # If not found, store and return None
   444	        self.info["chromatogram_count"] = None
   445	        return None
   446	
   447	
   448	if __name__ == "__main__":
   449	    print(__doc__)
   450

"""
Comprehensive test script for the get_chromatogram_count() fix.
"""

import pymzml
import os

def test_example_mzml():
    """Test with the original example file."""
    mzml_file_path = os.path.join('tests', 'data', 'example.mzML')
    
    print(f"Testing with file: {mzml_file_path}")
    
    try:
        reader = pymzml.run.Reader(mzml_file_path)
        print("Reader created successfully")
        
        # Get the counts (this will trigger lazy loading if needed)
        spectrum_count = reader.get_spectrum_count()
        chromatogram_count = reader.get_chromatogram_count()
        
        print(f"Spectrum count: {spectrum_count}")
        print(f"Chromatogram count: {chromatogram_count}")
        
        # Validate the values make sense
        assert spectrum_count == 2918, f"Expected 2918 spectra, got {spectrum_count}"
        assert chromatogram_count == 1, f"Expected 1 chromatogram, got {chromatogram_count}"
        
        print("✓ Example mzML test passed")
        return True
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"✗ Example mzML test failed: {e}")
        return False

def test_mini_chrom_mzml():
    """Test with a file that might have different characteristics."""
    mzml_file_path = os.path.join('tests', 'data', 'mini.chrom.mzML')
    
    if not os.path.exists(mzml_file_path):
        print(f"Skipping {mzml_file_path} - file not found")
        return True
    
    print(f"Testing with file: {mzml_file_path}")
    
    try:
        reader = pymzml.run.Reader(mzml_file_path)
        print("Reader created successfully")
        
        # Get the counts (this will trigger lazy loading if needed)
        spectrum_count = reader.get_spectrum_count()
        chromatogram_count = reader.get_chromatogram_count()
        
        print(f"Spectrum count: {spectrum_count}")
        print(f"Chromatogram count: {chromatogram_count}")
        
        print("✓ Mini chrom mzML test passed")
        return True
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"✗ Mini chrom mzML test failed: {e}")
        return False

def test_edge_cases():
    """Test edge cases."""
    print("Testing edge cases...")
    
    try:
        # Test that the method gracefully handles None values
        mzml_file_path = os.path.join('tests', 'data', 'example.mzML')
        reader = pymzml.run.Reader(mzml_file_path)
        
        # Both counts should be integers or None, not cause KeyErrors
        spectrum_count = reader.get_spectrum_count()
        chromatogram_count = reader.get_chromatogram_count()
        
        # They should be valid values (not KeyError)
        assert isinstance(spectrum_count, (int, type(None))), f"spectrum_count should be int or None, got {type(spectrum_count)}"
        assert isinstance(chromatogram_count, (int, type(None))), f"chromatogram_count should be int or None, got {type(chromatogram_count)}"
        
        print("✓ Edge case test passed")
        return True
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"✗ Edge case test failed: {e}")
        return False

def main():
    """Run all tests."""
    print("Running comprehensive tests for get_chromatogram_count() fix...\n")
    
    tests = [
        test_example_mzml,
        test_mini_chrom_mzml,
        test_edge_cases,
    ]
    
    passed = 0
    total = len(tests)
    
    for test in tests:
        if test():
            passed += 1
        print()
    
    print(f"Results: {passed}/{total} tests passed")
    
    if passed == total:
        print("🎉 All tests passed!")
        return True
    else:
        print("❌ Some tests failed!")
        return False

if __name__ == "__main__":
    success = main()
